'{"chunk_id":"39d742","wall_time_seconds":0.2904861,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1490,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\\r\\n * full sections.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\\r\\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\\r\\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\\r\\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\\r\\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\\r\\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\\r\\n * never collide:\\r\\n *\\r\\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug \\"mc-practice-1\\"\\r\\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug \\"guided::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug \\"drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>\\"\\r\\n *     homework       →  pool_slug \\"drill::assignment::<assignmentId>\\"\\r\\n *     everything else→  pool_slug \\"drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>\\"\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Official section reads must confirm the account or retain a paused device\\r\\n * copy. Other drill reads preserve the legacy missing-row fallback.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  loadPracticeSession,\\r\\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n  completePracticeSession,\\r\\n  migratePracticeSession,\\r\\n  practiceSessionIdFor,\\r\\n  PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\\r\\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\\r\\nimport { PracticeSession, TABLES } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { isActivePracticeTestDevice } from \'@/lib/practiceTestStore\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\\r\\nconst SECTION_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::`;\\r\\nconst TEST_LEG_PREFIX = `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}practice-test::`;\\r\\nconst accountSectionKey = (key: string) =>\\r\\n  key.startsWith(SECTION_PREFIX) || key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX);\\r\\nconst accountWriteChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction queueAccountWrite(userId: string, key: string, write: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const identity = `${userId}::${key}`;\\r\\n  const previous = accountWriteChains.get(identity) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const current = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(write);\\r\\n  accountWriteChains.set(identity, current);\\r\\n  void current.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (accountWriteChains.get(identity) === current) accountWriteChains.delete(identity);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return current;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertActiveTestLeg(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!key.startsWith(TEST_LEG_PREFIX)) return;\\r\\n  const runId = key.slice(TEST_LEG_PREFIX.length).split(\'::\')[0];\\r\\n  if (!await isActivePracticeTestDevice(userId, runId)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'This practice test is active on another device.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function readSectionFromAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\')\\r\\n    .select(\'data\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'table_name\', TABLES.practice_sessions)\\r\\n    .eq(\'record_id\', practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key))\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(data?.data);\\r\\n  return parsed.success ? migratePracticeSession(parsed.data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function syncSectionToAccount(userId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const record = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\\r\\n  if (!record) throw new Error(\'The section could not be saved.\');\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase.from(\'bootcamp_kv\').upsert({\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    table_name: TABLES.practice_sessions,\\r\\n    record_id: record.id,\\r\\n    data: record,\\r\\n  }, { onConflict: \'user_id,table_name,record_id\' });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\\r\\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst sectionBackupKey = (userId: string, key: string) =>\\r\\n  `lsatu:section-backup:${practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key)}`;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Synchronous device copy, including the latest unacknowledged choice. */\\r\\nexport function writeSectionDeviceBackup(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  key: string | null,\\r\\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\\r\\n): string | null {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !key || !accountSectionKey(key) || !snapshot.questionQueue.length) return null;\\r\\n  const previous = readSectionDeviceBackup(userId, key);\\r\\n  const time = new Date(Math.max(Date.now(), previous ? Date.parse(previous.updated_at) + 1 : 0)).toISOString();\\r\\n  const record = PracticeSession.safeParse({\\r\\n    id: practiceSessionIdFor(userId, key),\\r\\n    user_id: userId,\\r\\n    pool_slug: key,\\r\\n    completion_id: key,\\r\\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\\r\\n    question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,\\r\\n    current_index: Math.min(Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0), snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1),\\r\\n    attempts: snapshot.attempts,\\r\\n    status: \'in_progress\',\\r\\n    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs,\\r\\n    timer_mode: snapshot.timerMode,\\r\\n    timer_custom_minutes: snapshot.timerCustomMinutes,\\r\\n    regulation_answered_qids: snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids,\\r\\n    started_at: time,\\r\\n    updated_at: time,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (!record.success) return null;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    window.localStorage.setItem(sectionBackupKey(userId, key), JSON.stringify(record.data));\\r\\n    return time;\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\'[drill:section] device backup unavailable\', error);\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction readSectionDeviceBackup(userId: string, key: string): DrillResumeSession | null {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(sectionBackupKey(userId, key));\\r\\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null);\\r\\n    if (!parsed.success || parsed.data.user_id !== userId || parsed.data.pool_slug !== key) return null;\\r\\n    return parsed.data;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b6291c","wall_time_seconds":0.3814255,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":848,"output":"-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\n-- id is text, not uuid: newRunId()\'s fallback path does not produce a uuid.\\r\\ncreate table if not exists public.practice_test_runs (\\r\\n  id               text primary key,\\r\\n  user_id          uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  pt               int not null,\\r\\n  pace             text not null,\\r\\n  legs             jsonb not null,\\r\\n  current_leg      int not null default 0,\\r\\n  break_after_leg  int not null,\\r\\n  break_taken      boolean not null default false,\\r\\n  break_started_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  status           text not null check (status in (\'in-progress\', \'complete\', \'abandoned\')),\\r\\n  revision         int not null default 0,\\r\\n  completed_at     timestamptz,\\r\\n  raw_correct      int,\\r\\n  raw_total        int,\\r\\n  scaled_score     int,\\r\\n  score_source     text check (score_source in (\'official-table\', \'estimate\')),\\r\\n  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  -- Exists solely as the target of the composite FKs below. null,\\r\\n      practice_test_leg: config.practiceTestLegIndex ?? null,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return toSession(data as RcDrillSessionRow);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getSession(id: string): Promise<RcDrillSession | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).select().eq(\'id\', id).maybeSingle();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return data ? []) as RcDrillSessionRow[]).map(toSession);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst clockWriteChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Save spent time and, on navigation, the account-acknowledged question position. */\\r\\nexport function saveSessionClock(id: string, elapsedSeconds: number, currentQuestionIndex?: number): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const previous = clockWriteChains.get(id) ?? write.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (clockWriteChains.get(id) === write) clockWriteChains.delete(id);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return write;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function async function completeSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const session = await getSession(id);\\r\\n  if (!session || session.userId !== userId) raise(error);\\r\\n  if (!data) {\\r\\n    const latest = await getSession(id);\\r\\n    if (latest?.userId === userId && latest.status === \'completed\') return;\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'The completed section did not reach your account.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function setSessionExcluded(id: string, excluded: boolean): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({ excluded_from_analytics: excluded })\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', id);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function deleteSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).delete().eq(\'id\', id);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- attempts */\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Writes one attempt row. The first write for a question within a session is the\\r\\n * first attempt; every later write for that same question, review re-answers\\r\\n * included, is a new row flagged `is_first_attempt: false`. Existing rows are\\r\\n * never updated, so the original answer stays intact.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function recordAttempt(attempt: RcAttempt): Promise<RcAttempt> {\\r\\n  const userId = attempt.userId ?? (await requireUserId());\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(attempt.sessionId);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const existingAttempt = async (): Promise<RcAttempt | null> => {\\r\\n    if (!attempt.id) return null;\\r\\n    const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE).select()\\r\\n      .eq(\'id\', attempt.id).eq(\'user_id\', userId).maybeSingle();\\r\\n    raise(error);\\r\\n    if (!data) return null;\\r\\n    const saved = toAttempt(data as RcAttemptRow);\\r\\n    if (saved.sessionId !== attempt.sessionId || saved.questionId !== attempt.questionId\\r\\n      || saved.passageId !== attempt.passageId || saved.selected !== attempt.selected) {\\r\\n      throw new Error(\'The saved answer ID belongs to different work.\');\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return saved;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  // The client keeps this UUID in its device backup. A successful insert whose\\r\\n  // response was lost must be acknowledged as the same row on retry.\\r\\n  const alreadySaved = await existingAttempt();\\r\\n  if (alreadySaved) return alreadySaved;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: prior, error: priorError } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'id\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', attempt.sessionId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'question_id\', attempt.questionId)\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n  raise(priorError);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const isFirstAttempt = (prior ?? []).length === 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .insert({\\r\\n      ...(attempt.id ? { id: attempt.id } : {}),\\r\\n      session_id: attempt.sessionId,\\r\\n      user_id: userId,\\r\\n      question_id: attempt.questionId,\\r\\n      passage_id: attempt.passageId,\\r\\n      selected: attempt.selected,\\r\\n      is_correct: attempt.isCorrect,\\r\\n      time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\\r\\n      flagged: attempt.flagged,\\r\\n      eliminations: attempt.eliminations,\\r\\n      is_first_attempt: isFirstAttempt,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  if (error?.code === \'23505\') {\\r\\n    const saved = await existingAttempt();\\r\\n    if (saved) return saved;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return toAttempt(data as RcAttemptRow);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getSessionAttempts(sessionId: string): Promise<RcAttempt[]> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\\r\\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n"}'}]

\'@/features/rc/lib/rcBank\';\\r\\nimport type { RcPassage } from \'@/features/rc/lib/rcTypes\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport type { PracticeTestLeg, PracticeTestRun } from \'@/lib/practiceTest\';\\r\\nimport type { SectionQuestionStat } from \'@/lib/sectionHistorySave\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PracticeTestReviewQuestion {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  number: number;\\r\\n  type: string | null;\\r\\n  selected: string | null;\\r\\n  correctAnswer: string;\\r\\n  correct: boolean | null;\\r\\n  timeMs: number | null;\\r\\n  flagged: boolean | null;\\r\\n  blindReviewAnswer: string | null;\\r\\n  href: string;\\r\\n  rcContent?: {\\r\\n    passage: string;\\r\\n    prompt: string | null;\\r\\n    choices: { letter: string; text: string }[];\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PracticeTestLegReview {\\r\\n  legIndex: number;\\r\\n  questions: PracticeTestReviewQuestion[];\\r\\n  available: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst answer = (value: unknown): string | null =>\\r\\n  typeof value === \'string\' && /^[A-E]$/.test(value) ? value : null;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function mapLrReviewQuestions(raw: unknown, runId: string): PracticeTestReviewQuestion[] {\\r\\n  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return [];\\r\\n  return raw.flatMap((value: SectionQuestionStat) => {\\r\\n    if (!value || typeof value.qid !== \'string\' || !/^PT\\\\d+-S\\\\d+-Q\\\\d+$/.test(value.qid)) return [];\\r\\n    const correctAnswer = answer(value.correctAnswer);\\r\\n    if (!correctAnswer) return [];\\r\\n    return [{\\r\\n      id: value.qid,\\r\\n      number: Number.isFinite(value.qnum) ? value.qnum : Number(value.qid.match(/-Q(\\\\d+)$/)?.[1] ?? 0),\\r\\n      type: typeof value.qtype === \'string\' ? value.qtype : null,\\r\\n      selected: answer(value.initialAnswer),\\r\\n      correctAnswer,\\r\\n      correct: typeof value.initialCorrect === \'boolean\' ? value.initialCorrect : null,\\r\\n      timeMs: typeof value.timeMs === \'number\' && value.timeMs >= 0 ? value.timeMs : null,\\r\\n      flagged: typeof value.flagged === \'boolean\' ? value.flagged : null,\\r\\n      blindReviewAnswer: answer(value.brAnswer),\\r\\n      href: `/drill?mode=type-drill&qids=${encodeURIComponent(value.qid)}&present=smart&returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(`/testing/test/${runId}`)}`,\\r\\n    }];\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ntype FirstRcAttempt = { question_id: number; selected: string | null; is_correct: boolean | null; time_ms: number; flagged: boolean };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function mapRcReviewQuestions(passages: RcPassage[], attempts: FirstRcAttempt[], sessionId: string): PracticeTestReviewQuestion[] {\\r\\n  const first = new Map(attempts.map((attempt) => [attempt.question_id, attempt]));\\r\\n  return passages.flatMap((passage) => passage.questions.map((question) => {\\r\\n    const attempt = first.get(question.questionId);\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      id: String(question.questionId),\\r\\n      number: question.position,\\r\\n      type: question.questionType || null,\\r\\n      selected: answer(attempt?.selected),\\r\\n      correctAnswer: question.correctAnswer,\\r\\n      correct: typeof attempt?.is_correct === \'boolean\' ? attempt.is_correct : null,\\r\\n      timeMs: typeof attempt?.time_ms === \'number\' && attempt.time_ms >= 0 ? attempt.time_ms : null,\\r\\n      flagged: typeof attempt?.flagged === \'boolean\' ? attempt.flagged : null,\\r\\n      blindReviewAnswer: null,\\r\\n      href: `/rc/drill?sessionId=${encodeURIComponent(sessionId)}&review=1&q=${question.questionId}`,\\r\\n      rcContent: {\\r\\n        passage: passage.text,\\r\\n        prompt: question.prompt,\\r\\n        choices: question.choices.map(({ letter, text }) => ({ letter, text })),\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    } satisfies PracticeTestReviewQuestion;\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function readLrReview(userId: string, run: PracticeTestRun, leg: PracticeTestLeg, legIndex: number): Promise<PracticeTestLegReview> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'section_history\')\\r\\n    .select(\'questions_json\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'practice_test_run_id\', run.id)\\r\\n    .eq(\'practice_test_leg\', legIndex)\\r\\n    .eq(\'pt\', run.pt)\\r\\n    .eq(\'section\', leg.section)\\r\\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const questions = mapLrReviewQuestions(data?.[0]?.questions_json, run.id);\\r\\n  return { legIndex, questions, available: questions.length > 0 };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function readRcReview(userId: string, run: PracticeTestRun, leg: PracticeTestLeg, legIndex: number): Promise<PracticeTestLegReview> {\\r\\n  const { data: session, error: sessionError } = await supabase.from(\'rc_drill_sessions\')\\r\\n    .select(\'id, passage_ids, status\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'practice_test_run_id\', run.id)\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"77e152","wall_time_seconds":0.1864798,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1676,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Practice tests: a full sitting, assembled from real sections.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A **practice test** here is what the modern LSAT is — three scored sections,\\r\\n * two Logical Reasoning and one Reading Comprehension, each run under its own\\r\\n * 35:00 clock, with a break in the middle. It is not a long drill: the\\r\\n * composition is fixed, the order is the published one, and nothing is revealed\\r\\n * until the whole sitting is over.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The run is saved to the student\'s account by practiceTestStore. A device\\r\\n * copy remains available as a recovery aid. Each leg still uses the existing\\r\\n * question runner (`/drill` for LR, `/rc` for RC).\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport type { QuestionManifest } from \'@/lib/questionLoader\';\\r\\nimport type { RcSection } from \'@/features/rc/lib/rcSections\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Section clock at standard pace. The same 35:00 both section kinds run. */\\r\\nexport const PRACTICE_TEST_SECTION_SECONDS = 35 * 60;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The break the real test gives you, after the second scored section. */\\r\\nexport const PRACTICE_TEST_BREAK_SECONDS = 10 * 60;\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * An LR section below this is a partial capture, not a section. The bank\\r\\n * publishes only questions carrying a written explanation, so a short section\\r\\n * would misreport pacing rather than measure it. Never pad one to reach it.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const LR_FULL_SECTION_MIN_QUESTIONS = 24;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PracticeTestPaceId = \'standard\' | \'time-and-a-half\' | \'double-time\' | \'untimed\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * One spelling per accommodation, for the whole room.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This door said \\"Time & a Half\\" and \\"Double Time\\" while the section door said\\r\\n * \\"Time and a half\\" and \\"Double time\\", so the same two accommodations were\\r\\n * spelled two ways and cased two ways depending on which door a student opened.\\r\\n * Settled on the section door\'s form because AccommodationNote already says\\r\\n * \\"You have time and a half\\" and \\"You have double time\\" in prose: spelled out,\\r\\n * sentence case. The odd one out was this list.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst PACE_LABELS: Record<PracticeTestPaceId, string> = {\\r\\n  standard: \'Standard\',\\r\\n  \'time-and-a-half\': \'Time and a half\',\\r\\n  \'double-time\': \'Double time\',\\r\\n  untimed: \'Untimed\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Each pace as a percentage of the student\'s OWN standard clock, not of 35:00.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * These were absolute before: \'standard\' was hard-wired to 35 minutes whatever\\r\\n * the student\'s standing timing mode said. A student carrying extra time who\\r\\n * picked \\"Standard\\" was handed a 35-minute section, which is less time than\\r\\n * they are entitled to, across a whole two-hour sitting. The section door has\\r\\n * always read the setting (SectionSelector\'s baseMinutes); the practice test\\r\\n * door never did, so the same two words meant two different clocks in one room.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * 100% is whatever their standing default is, so \\"Standard\\" means \\"my normal\\r\\n * section\\" for everyone.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst PACE_PERCENT: Record<PracticeTestPaceId, number | null> = {\\r\\n  standard: 100,\\r\\n  \'time-and-a-half\': 150,\\r\\n  \'double-time\': 200,\\r\\n  untimed: null,\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The pace\'s display name. The clock it implies depends on the student. */\\r\\nexport function practiceTestPaceLabel(id: PracticeTestPaceId): string {\\r\\n  return PACE_LABELS[id] ?? PACE_LABELS.standard;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Seconds one section runs for at this pace, off the student\'s own base. */\\r\\nexport function practiceTestSectionSeconds(\\r\\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\\r\\n  baseSeconds: number = PRACTICE_TEST_SECTION_SECONDS,\\r\\n): number | null {\\r\\n  // Not `??`: untimed\'s percent IS null, and a nullish fallback would quietly\\r\\n  // turn \\"no clock\\" into the standard one. Only an unrecognised id falls back.\\r\\n  const percent = pace in PACE_PERCENT ? PACE_PERCENT[pace] : PACE_PERCENT.standard;\\r\\n  if (percent === null) return null;\\r\\n  return Math.round((baseSeconds * percent) / 100);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The pace options for a student whose own standard section is `baseSeconds`. */\\r\\nexport function practiceTestPaces(baseSeconds: number = PRACTICE_TEST_SECTION_SECONDS): {\\r\\n  id: PracticeTestPaceId;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  /** Seconds per section. Null when untimed. */\\r\\n  sectionSeconds: number | null;\\r\\n}[] {\\r\\n  return (Object.keys(PACE_LABELS) as PracticeTestPaceId[]).map((id) => ({\\r\\n    id,\\r\\n    label: PACE_LABELS[id],\\r\\n    sectionSeconds: practiceTestSectionSeconds(id, baseSeconds),\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What a run\'s sections actually run for. Reads the seconds resolved when the\\r\\n * run was created rather than recomputing, so changing the timing setting\\r\\n * halfway through a two-hour sitting cannot move the clock under it. Runs\\r\\n * created before this was stored fall back to the old absolute reading.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run: PracticeTestRun): number | null {\\r\\n  return run.sectionSeconds !== undefined\\r\\n    ? run.sectionSeconds\\r\\n    : practiceTestSectionSeconds(run.pace);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PracticeTestLegKind = \'lr\' | \'rc\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PracticeTestLeg {\\r\\n  kind: PracticeTestLegKind;\\r\\n  /** The section number it carried on the real PrepTest. */\\r\\n  section: number;\\r\\n  /** Questions in this leg — what it contributes to the raw score. */\\r\\n  questionCount: number;\\r\\n  /** RC only: the four passages, in published order. */\\r\\n  passageIds?: number[];\\r\\n  /** ISO stamp set when the student begins this leg. */\\r\\n  startedAt?: string;\\r\\n  /** ISO stamp set when its result is read back. */\\r\\n  completedAt?: string;\\r\\n  correct?: number;\\r\\n  answered?: number;\\r\\n  timeMs?: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PracticeTestStatus = \'in-progress\' | \'complete\' | \'abandoned\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PracticeTestRun {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  /** Account owner. Older device-only runs may not have this field. */\\r\\n  userId?: string;\\r\\n  /** Server revision used for conditional writes across devices. */\\r\\n  revision?: number;\\r\\n  /** The browser currently allowed to write this run. */\\r\\n  activeDeviceId?: string | null;\\r\\n  updatedAt?: string;\\r\\n  pt: number;\\r\\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Seconds per section for this sitting, resolved against the student\'s own\\r\\n   * standard clock when the run was created. Optional because runs stored\\r\\n   * before it existed do not carry it; read it through\\r\\n   * `practiceTestRunSectionSeconds`, never directly.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  sectionSeconds?: number | null;\\r\\n  legs: PracticeTestLeg[];\\r\\n  /** Index of the leg the student is on. Equals legs.length when finished. */\\r\\n  currentLeg: number;\\r\\n  /** Legs completed before the break. The real test breaks after two. */\\r\\n  breakAfterLeg: number;\\r\\n  /** True once the student has taken (or skipped) the break. */\\r\\n  breakTaken: boolean;\\r\\n  /** ISO stamp set when the break clock started, so it survives a reload. */\\r\\n  breakStartedAt?: string;\\r\\n  /** Device backup of the clock at a failed break exit. Never a running clock. */\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"4467e9","wall_time_seconds":0.4458204,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":943,"output":"\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Close out one leg of a practice test.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Order is load-bearing: attempts, then the journal, then section_history,\\r\\n   * then the resume record. The invigilator reads a section_history row as\\r\\n   * \\"this leg is finished\\" and advances past it, so writing history before the\\r\\n   * journal is complete would make the journal retry unreachable. And the\\r\\n   * resume record is finalized only after history lands, or a failed persist\\r\\n   * would leave a completed resume row that re-entry rejects, stranding a leg\\r\\n   * that can never be scored.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const runSittingCloseout = async (\\r\\n    attempts: DrillSession[\'attempts\'],\\r\\n    finishedSession: DrillSession,\\r\\n  ) => {\\r\\n    if (ptRun && user && !await isActivePracticeTestDevice(user.id, ptRun.runId)) {\\r\\n      setHandoffLost(true);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const back = drillReturnTo || \'/classroom\';\\r\\n    const key =\\r\\n      user && ptRun\\r\\n        ? closeoutKey(user.id, ptRun.runId, ptRun.legIndex)\\r\\n        : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      if (!user) throw new Error(\'sitting closeout without a signed-in user\');\\r\\n      if (!ptRun) {\\r\\n        // A sitting whose launch state lost its identity is a bug, not a case\\r\\n        // to paper over with a guessed key.\\r\\n        throw new Error(\'sitting closeout without run identity\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const stats = buildSectionStats(finishedSession, undefined);\\r\\n      const expectedWaj = wajQidsForSection(stats);\\r\\n\\r\\n      const { savedQids, wajQids } = await commitSessionAttempts(\\r\\n        attempts,\\r\\n        \'full-section\',\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      let progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { savedQids, wajQids });\\r\\n\\r\\n      // commitSessionAttempts journals only ANSWERED misses. The unanswered\\r\\n      // ones are misses too, and nothing else records them once the results\\r\\n      // screen stops rendering.\\r\\n      const owed = outstandingWajQids(expectedWaj, progress);\\r\\n      if (owed.length > 0) {\\r\\n        const written = await logSectionWrongAnswers({\\r\\n          stats,\\r\\n          qids: owed,\\r\\n          classId,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { wajQids: written });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Incomplete journal means the leg is not finished. Leave the key, write\\r\\n      // no history row, and let re-entry retry only what is still owed.\\r\\n      const stillOwed = outstandingWajQids(expectedWaj, progress);\\r\\n      if (stillOwed.length > 0) {\\r\\n        console.error(\'[drill:sitting-closeout] journal incomplete\', {\\r\\n          runId: ptRun.runId,\\r\\n          legIndex: ptRun.legIndex,\\r\\n          owed: stillOwed.length,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!progress.historySaved) {\\r\\n        const sectionHistoryId = await persistSectionResults({\\r\\n          session: finishedSession,\\r\\n          stats,\\r\\n          classId,\\r\\n          userId: user.id,\\r\\n          practiceTestRun: ptRun,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        // Every leg of a practice test is a section, and each one gets its own\\r\\n        // report. Not awaited: the leg is saved, and the sitting has to be able\\r\\n        // to move on to the next section whatever the mail server is doing.\\r\\n        void emailSectionReport(sectionHistoryId);\\r\\n        progress = recordCloseoutProgress(key!, { historySaved: true });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      await finalizeDrillResumeSession(attempts);\\r\\n      clearCloseoutProgress(key!);\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      console.error(\'[drill:sitting-closeout] failed\', error);\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      navigate(back);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const handleFinishSection = async (\\r\\n    attemptsOverride?: DrillSession[\'attempts\'],\\r\\n    options?: { returnToAfterFinish?: string },\\r\\n  ) => {\\r\\n    if (!session) return;\\r\\n    if (ptRun && user && !await isActivePracticeTestDevice(user.id, ptRun.runId)) {\\r\\n      setHandoffLost(true);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"24d0f4","wall_time_seconds":0.6953488,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1234,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Persisting the result of a full LR section.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This used to live inside `LRSectionResults`, in a mount effect. That was fine\\r\\n * while the only way to finish a section was to look at the results screen, and\\r\\n * wrong the moment a practice test needed to finish a section WITHOUT showing\\r\\n * one: the sitting\'s scorer (`readLrLegResult`) reads back the `section_history`\\r\\n * row that effect wrote, so skipping the screen skipped the row and every leg\\r\\n * read as unfinished.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * So the writing lives here and the screen calls it, rather than the other way\\r\\n * round.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Two behaviours are deliberate and differ from the old inline code:\\r\\n *\\r\\n * 1. `user_id` is set explicitly. The column is nullable with no default and no\\r\\n *    trigger, the old insert omitted it, and `readLrLegResult` filters on it —\\r\\n *    so LR leg readback almost certainly never matched in production.\\r\\n * 2. `persistSectionResults` THROWS on failure. The results screen catches and\\r\\n *    toasts (a standalone student can retry by other means), but the practice\\r\\n *    test closeout must be able to tell that the write failed, because it\\r\\n *    decides whether the leg counts as finished.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport type { Json } from \'@/integrations/supabase/types\';\\r\\nimport { questionBank } from \'@/lib/questionLoader\';\\r\\nimport type { DrillSession, BlindReviewResult } from \'@/types/drill\';\\r\\nimport { analyseSection, type AnalysisInput } from \'@/lib/sectionAnalysis\';\\r\\nimport { UNANSWERED } from \'@/lib/sectionAnswer\';\\r\\nexport { UNANSWERED } from \'@/lib/sectionAnswer\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One question\'s outcome in a sat section. */\\r\\nexport interface SectionQuestionStat {\\r\\n  qid: string;\\r\\n  qnum: number;\\r\\n  qtype: string;\\r\\n  difficulty: number;\\r\\n  initialAnswer: string;\\r\\n  correctAnswer: string;\\r\\n  initialCorrect: boolean;\\r\\n  brAnswer?: string;\\r\\n  brCorrect?: boolean;\\r\\n  brChanged?: boolean;\\r\\n  timeMs: number;\\r\\n  brTimeMs?: number;\\r\\n  switchCount: number;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * What the bank knows about the question, copied onto the sitting so the\\r\\n   * record survives the bank changing under it. A report read next year has to\\r\\n   * describe the question as it was when it was sat, and re-reading the bank at\\r\\n   * display time cannot promise that.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  reasoningTypes?: string[];\\r\\n  questionFeatures?: string[];\\r\\n  /** How many times the student opened it, and how many of those changed nothing. */\\r\\n  visitCount?: number;\\r\\n  noChangeRevisits?: number;\\r\\n  /** Choices crossed off. Three of them is \\"down to two\\", the flag criterion. */\\r\\n  eliminations?: string[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Did they ever open it? A question worked and left blank is a decision; one\\r\\n   * never reached is the clock. Both are \\"unanswered\\" without this.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  everVisited?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Every choice put down on this question, in order, each stamped with how\\r\\n   * far into the question it was made. The results screen draws it as a\\r\\n   * timeline; an unanswered question has none.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  answerTrail?: Array<{ answer: string; atMs: number }>;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Answered after the section clock ran out. Undefined for a section that\\r\\n   * never ran out, which is not the same as false on one that did.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  answeredInOvertime?: boolean;\\r\\n  flagged: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-question stats for a sat section, in the order the questions were served.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Pure, and shared by the results screen and the practice-test closeout so the\\r\\n * two can never compute a different row for the same section.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function buildSectionStats(\\r\\n  session: DrillSession,\\r\\n  brResults?: BlindReviewResult[],\\r\\n): SectionQuestionStat[] {\\r\\n  // Who was already answered at the buzzer. A section that never ran out has\\r\\n  // no mark, and no question on it is in overtime.\\r\\n  const atBuzzer = session.regulationMark\\r\\n    ? new Set(session.regulationMark.answeredQids)\\r\\n    : null;\\r\\n  const visited = new Set(session.visitedQids ?? []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return session.questionQueue\\r\\n    .map((qid, idx) => {\\r\\n      const question = questionBank.getQuestion(qid);\\r\\n      const attempt = session.attempts.get(qid);\\r\\n      const brResult = brResults?.find((br) => br.qid === qid);\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!question) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // No attempt means the question was never answered, which counts as wrong.\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        qid,\\r\\n        qnum: idx + 1,\\r\\n        qtype: question.qtype,\\r\\n        difficulty: question.difficulty,\\r\\n        initialAnswer: attempt?.selectedAnswer || UNANSWERED,\\r\\n        correctAnswer: question.correctAnswer,\\r\\n        initialCorrect: attempt?.correct || false,\\r\\n        brAnswer: brResult?.brAnswer,\\r\\n        brCorrect: brResult?.correct,\\r\\n        brChanged: brResult?.brChanged,\\r\\n        timeMs: attempt?.timeMs || 0,\\r\\n        brTimeMs: brResult?.brTimeMs,\\r\\n        switchCount: attempt?.switchCount || 0,\\r\\n        reasoningTypes: question.reasoningType?.length ? question.reasoningType : undefined,\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7a71de","wall_time_seconds":0.6862363,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":372,"output":"\\r\\n-- True when this user may start a sitting of this PrepTest: the Practice tests\\r\\n-- switch, an admin, or a live assignment for exactly this PT.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_sit_practice_test(_user uuid, _pt integer)\\r\\n RETURNS boolean\\r\\n LANGUAGE sql\\r\\n STABLE\\r\\n SECURITY DEFINER\\r\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\r\\nAS $function$\\r\\n  SELECT\\r\\n    public.has_role(_user, \'admin\')\\r\\n    OR EXISTS (\\r\\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles\\r\\n      WHERE class_id = _user::text AND has_practice_access\\r\\n    )\\r\\n    OR EXISTS (\\r\\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.practice_test_assignments\\r\\n      WHERE student_id = _user AND pt = _pt AND status <> \'archived\'\\r\\n        AND (unlock_at IS NULL OR unlock_at <= now())\\r\\n    );\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- The API cannot be used to start a sitting the student was not given.\\r\\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS pt_runs_owner_insert ON public.practice_test_runs;\\r\\nCREATE POLICY pt_runs_owner_insert\\r\\n  ON public.practice_test_runs FOR INSERT\\r\\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid() AND public.can_sit_practice_test(auth.uid(), pt));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- An assigned sitting shows on Assigned work like everything else.\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS classroom_items_type_check;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.classroom_items ADD CONSTRAINT classroom_items_type_check\\r\\n  CHECK (type = ANY (ARRAY[\'drill\', \'homework\', \'bootcamp_module\', \'note\', \'session_recap\',\\r\\n    \'announcement\', \'upload_summary\', \'weekly_plan_task\', \'smartboard_recap\', \'activity\',\\r\\n    \'practice_test\']));\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\\r\\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\\r\\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. Returns null for\\r\\n * the surfaces that own their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for\\r\\n * anything launched from an explicit list of qids, where the caller already\\r\\n * knows the set.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function drillResumeKey(input: {\\r\\n  mode: string;\\r\\n  config?: unknown;\\r\\n  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\\r\\n  /** The classroom homework assignment this run belongs to, when it does. */\\r\\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Full exam legs must never share the standalone section\'s resume row. */\\r\\n  ptRun?: { runId: string; legIndex: number } | null;\\r\\n}): string | null {\\r\\n  if (input.ptRun) {\\r\\n    return `${TEST_LEG_PREFIX}${input.ptRun.runId}::leg-${input.ptRun.legIndex}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (input.assignmentId) {\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}assignment::${input.assignmentId}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const mode = input.mode;\\r\\n  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\\r\\n    if (!config) return null;\\r\\n    const pt = config.pt;\\r\\n    const section = config.section;\\r\\n    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\\r\\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\\r\\n    if (!config) return null;\\r\\n    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\\r\\n    if (selected?.length) {\\r\\n      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\\r\\n      // same run, a different list is a different one.\\r\\n      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const shape = JSON.stringify({\\r\\n      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\\r\\n      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\\r\\n      pts: config.pts ?? [],\\r\\n      sections: config.sections ?? [],\\r\\n      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-connected-experience/docs/implementation-plans/2026-09-30-timed-write-implementation.md\n+# Ship the atomic PrepTest writer before standalone timed work\n+\n+Date: 2026-09-30\n+\n+Status: **Implementation design only. No SQL, RPC, frontend switch, or backend deployment is implemented by this document.** Ticket #167 remains open. This expands [the proposed timed-write contract](2026-09-30-timed-write-contract.md); that contract's live metadata evidence and deployment approval requirements still apply.\n+\n+The first backend release should cover PrepTest parent state, LR legs, RC legs, and the break together. Standalone timed LR/RC follows as a separate adoption phase. A parent-only RPC would leave the child-write race intact.\n+\n+## The existing boundaries determine the first batch\n+\n+| Existing boundary | Current behavior | Required v1 replacement |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `src/lib/practiceTestStore.ts` | Parent creation, revision CAS, device claim, pending device copies | Versioned create/claim commands and authoritative command responses; existing reads and backup ownership checks remain |\n+| `src/lib/drillResumeSession.ts` | Separate parent-device check, then official LR KV snapshot upsert | LR snapshot checkpoint under the parent lock; retained device copy until acknowledgement |\n+| `src/pages/Drill.tsx`, `src/lib/sectionHistorySave.ts` | LR closeout writes attempts, WAJ, history, resume completion in separate requests | One official close-leg command; explicit projection completion contract |\n+| `src/features/rc/api/rcProgress.ts` | Session creation, answer insert, clock/position update, and completion each check/write separately | Typed RC start/checkpoint/close-leg commands |\n+| `src/features/rc/pages/RcPlayer.tsx` | Questions and clock gated on account reads/saves; closeout writes blank first-attempt rows | Consume acknowledged session/position/clock; closeout batch and parent advance commit together |\n+| `src/pages/PracticeTestRun.tsx` | Reads child results and later saves parent advancement/break/final score | Read authoritative parent returned by close-leg; parent and break commands cannot accept arbitrary JSON patches |\n+| `src/lib/practiceTestRunner.ts`, `src/lib/practiceTestReview.ts` | Read attributed historical LR/RC records | Preserve result/review reads; v1 completion is not inferred from a child row that has not committed parent advancement |\n+\n+Repository schema is concrete but is not a fresh live DDL verification. `practice_test_runs.id` is **text**, not UUID. Its statuses are `in-progress`, `complete`, `abandoned`; RC uses `in_progress`, `completed`, `abandoned`. LR official records are JSON in `bootcamp_kv`, under `practice_sessions`; do not assume a normalized LR sessions table exists. Keep the installed owner FKs and per-leg/first-attempt unique indexes described in the contract.\n+\n+## Phase 0: freeze behavior and reproduce the races\n+\n+Prepare an ordinary-authenticated two-client harness against an isolated database with the installed schema. Refresh the live metadata inventory before deployment. Exercise existing parent CAS, then demonstrate that separate child preflight/writes can race takeover. No service-role success is permission evidence.\n+\n+Record these current semantics before implementation:\n+\n+- Parent `section_seconds` freezes the user's chosen timing. The break is 600 seconds. Historical null timing retains existing reader compatibility.\n+- LR permits overtime and records `regulation_answered_qids`. Preserve that distinction; do not silently introduce automatic submission at budget expiry.\n+- RC answer column is `selected`; null closeout rows count as unanswered, and session elapsed time is not the sum of per-question times.\n+- Current scoring is client-derived. This work protects transactional ownership, clock state, and acknowledged results. It does **not** introduce a new secure-grade product requirement or claim server-verified answer correctness. Validate result structure and derive parent totals from acknowledged child results. Preserve existing scoring formulas and explicitly retain their trust boundary.\n+- Current LR closeout requires its expected WAJ work before the leg is considered finished. Do not move it after advancement without a separately recorded change to that behavior.\n+\n+## Phase 1: add protected control state and receipts\n+\n+Prepare two forward migrations, not a historical-record rewrite:\n+\n+1. `supabase/migrations/<timestamp>_timed_work_v1_control.sql`: control fields, receipt table, typed RPC helpers, and owner result reads.\n+2. `supabase/migrations/<timestamp>_timed_work_v1_enforcement.sql`: v1 direct-write restrictions and exhaustive OLD/NEW attribution protection.\n+\n+The exact timestamps are assigned when implementation starts. Keep deployment of enforcement and adapter activation coordinated; an RPC left beside unrestricted protected raw writes does not provide the guarantee.\n+\n+For the PT phase add nullable `timed_contract_version` to `practice_test_runs`, plus server-owned writer epoch, phase, accumulated elapsed milliseconds, running anchor, lease expiry, and active clock leg. Keep existing `revision` as the single command CAS version. Preserve existing device ID for UI display; possession of an arbitrary device ID alone does not authorize a write. Epoch/lease/revision are validated under the parent lock.\n+\n+Constraints for protected rows:\n+\n+- Version is supported; revision/epoch/elapsed are nonnegative.\n+- Running phases have an anchor and lease; paused/terminal phases have no running anchor.\n+- Clock leg matches the current frozen leg, or the phase is the one permitted break.\n+- Owner, PT, pace, section duration, frozen leg definition, and protected version cannot be changed by a client patch.\n+- Terminal state cannot reopen. No protected source or completion record is deleted by this API.\n+\n+Use a companion binding table for LR KV records. Its owner/run/leg, namespace, record ID, and frozen queue identity are immutable, and there is one binding per PT LR leg. The existing key `drill::practice-test::<runId>::leg-<index>` and `practiceSessionIdFor` can be preserved for PT compatibility. A generic owner JSON blob must not become writer/access authority.\n+\n+Use a durable command receipt table with unique `(owner_id, work_kind, work_id, command_id)`, canonical payload hash, committed revision, and committed result. A command UUID is separate from an RC attempt UUID. Store receipts in the same transaction as progress. Authenticated clients can read their own receipts but cannot insert/update/delete them directly. Retain receipts long enough for supported recovery; a TTL policy is not part of this batch.\n+\n+## Phase 2: implement one typed PT transaction\n+\n+Public entry points: `create_timed_practice_test_v1` and `commit_timed_work_v1`. Creation cannot take an arbitrary owner. New protected children are created inside start-leg, not by direct browser inserts.\n+\n+The commit envelope is `{workKind, workId, expectedRevision, writerEpoch, deviceId, commandId, operation, payload}`. Derive owner from `auth.uid()`. Parse only the fields for the named operation and reject unknown/immutable patches. Start with PT operations: claim/adopt, start-leg, checkpoint, pause, resume, close-leg, start-break, finish-break, abandon. Final completion is derived when the final leg closes; there is no unrestricted set-score or set-current-leg operation.\n+\n+Each successful command performs these steps:\n+\n+1. Resolve the actor-owned parent and lock it `FOR UPDATE`. Look for the command receipt under that lock. A matching receipt acknowledges the old committed operation before stale-revision or current-access rejection; changed payload rejects. Return both the receipt and **current** authoritative parent so retrying an old receipt cannot restore an older UI state.\n+2. Validate supported version, expected revision, writer epoch, lease, exact phase/leg, and immutable child binding. A new stale command returns a typed conflict with current state and makes no progress writes.\n+3. Check current PT entitlement using the existing `can_sit_practice_test` rules. Lock the actual grant row used for authorization: [REDACTED] profile feature row, exact owned/unlocked/nonarchived assignment, or admin-role row. A boolean precheck alone does not serialize a concurrent revoke. Use deterministic lock order and test it against the revocation paths. Missing access rejects new writes while preserving history and receipts.\n+4. Settle clock time using server time, bounded by the acknowledged lease expiry, then apply the operation. Never decrease elapsed time. Heartbeat renews only a valid active writer. Proposed 15-second heartbeat/45-second lease remains a proposal to verify, not an adopted product constant.\n+5. Persist LR snapshot or RC answer batch together with elapsed time and position. Validate qid membership, queue order, question count, selected letters/nulls, index bounds, and exact parent/leg/session attribution. Do not regenerate missing content or accept a new queue during recovery. Keep all acknowledged choices through takeover.\n+6. On close-leg, persist the official child result, required closeout projections, child terminal state, parent leg result, and advance/break/final state in one transaction. Increment parent revision once, write receipt, and return parent plus child checkpoint and server time.\n+\n+RC retains existing first-attempt uniqueness. Same attempt UUID with the same owner/session/question/passage/choice is idempotent; different content rejects. A different first-attempt UUID for an already committed question conflicts rather than replacing the original. Review reanswers require their separate allowed semantics and cannot reopen an exam leg. Clock checkpoint and answer acknowledgement must share the command; current split calls are not enough.\n+\n+LR stores the frozen `PracticeSession` shape and validates stable attribution outside that JSON. Do not trust supplied `user_id`, `pool_slug`, `completion_id`, or arbitrary section-history parent links. Keep unanswered/answered/overtime distinctions and current score trust. Validate totals against the frozen queue and derive parent aggregation from the acknowledged child record rather than a later unrelated readback.\n+\n+For LR WAJ/analytics, inventory `commitSessionAttempts`, `logSectionWrongAnswers`, and `persistSectionResults` before moving their writes. Either put mandatory rows in the transaction, or introduce an explicit closeout-pending phase with an idempotent transactionally enqueued projection job. In the latter design, parent advancement still waits for required projection acknowledgement. Mail remains optional and cannot block the official leg, matching current behavior. Do not leave the existing client closeout path writing official rows around the RPC.\n+\n+## Phase 3: close direct-write bypasses before switching clients\n+\n+Protected parent INSERT/UPDATE, attributed LR KV INSERT/UPDATE, RC session/attempt INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, and official section-result INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE must reject ordinary authenticated direct mutations. Restrict metadata-only RC operations deliberately rather than accidentally disabling exclusions/review flags. Preserve ordinary bootcamp data and historical reads.\n+\n+Enforcement checks **OLD and NEW** protection, owner, source identity, and attribution. Clearing the PT FK, setting contract version null, moving a protected KV record, or inserting an alternate child for the same protected leg cannot escape it. Reserved PT LR source keys cannot be recreated through generic KV writes. Replace conflicting permissive policies; adding a stricter permissive policy leaves the existing owner policy effective.\n+\n+Prefer a dedicated tightly privileged no-login executor role for protected RPCs if Lovable's managed migration role supports that ownership setup. Authenticated/anonymous must have no membership in it. Security-definer functions fix search path, qualify tables, check `auth.uid()`, and receive only narrow grants. Ordinary callers must not be able to forge an RPC-only bypass with a custom setting. Verify managed-role capabilities before selecting an enforcement technique; do not equate every `postgres`-owned definer function with this authorized writer.\n+\n+## Phase 4: switch PT adapters and explicitly adopt active work\n+\n+Add `src/lib/timedWorkClient.ts` for typed command envelopes/responses and conflict/access/expired-writer errors. Backups retain the same pending command UUID, payload, expected revision, and epoch until acknowledged. Never mint a fresh command on a lost-response retry.\n+\n+Switch the existing boundaries in the first table. The parent returned by close-leg drives advancement; `PracticeTestRun.tsx` stops performing a second parent save for that v1 leg. `RcPlayer.tsx` and `Drill.tsx` stop clocks and hide/gate questions on loss of authoritative writer state until claim/recovery is acknowledged. Break pause/resume/finish uses the same contract. Device elapsed time is recovery evidence, not server proof of an instantaneous offline pause.\n+\n+New PTs use v1 only after backend readiness is verified. Existing active rows enter through explicit adoption/claim under the parent lock. Validate and preserve owner, current leg, frozen queue/passages, acknowledged answers, saved position, timing, and terminal results. Adopt only a known server-owned child; unknown or contradictory device-only copies need explicit recovery rather than automatic import. Already completed/abandoned rows remain readable and are not rewritten.\n+\n+An old browser meeting an adopted record must fail its raw writes and require refresh. Compatibility tests must include bypass attempts through every legacy writer, not only the new adapter's happy path.\n+\n+## Phase 5: give standalone LR/RC durable sitting identities\n+\n+Do not include this in the initial PT rollout. Standalone LR currently uses `drill::section::ptX-sY` with a deterministic record ID; retakes overwrite that identity. A control row keyed only to it would confuse terminal history with a new sitting.\n+\n+Introduce a distinct v1 sitting UUID and immutable control/source identity. Use a distinct v1 LR KV record per sitting plus a current-active lookup, or normalized canonical snapshot storage. A unique active `(owner, source_kind, source_key)` permits one active writer while retaining completed retakes. RC already has a session UUID, but it still needs frozen standalone source authorization and control binding.\n+\n+Inventory every supported standalone source before enforcement: feature-granted manual full section, exact classroom/homework assignment, bootcamp source, and any supported custom timed source. Freeze source identity and check its current ownership/unlock/archive state on every command. Unsupported source kinds fail explicitly. Broad access can authorize its supported source kinds; an unrelated assignment cannot authorize an arbitrary queue. Retain existing timing/retake behavior and clearly identify any temporarily excluded legacy paths.\n+\n+## Code prepared is separate from deployed and verified\n+\n+Implementation deliverables before approval:\n+\n+- Reviewed additive schema/RPC and enforcement migrations, with immutable/terminal/owner constraints and a rollback-free forward recovery plan.\n+- An isolated PostgreSQL transaction harness covering same-revision races, save-versus-takeover, save-versus-revoke, changed-payload replay, old-receipt replay after later work, lease expiry, failed closeout, and raw OLD/NEW attribution bypasses.\n+- Frontend adapter tests for pending command retention, authoritative response consumption, no duplicate advancement, older clients, adoption, failed hydration, and frozen queues.\n+- Typecheck/build and student/teacher browser evidence before frontend release.\n+\n+Refresh live metadata immediately before deployment. Joshua must confirm the Lovable Cloud backend route and immediately approve any credit-using action. No Supabase CLI Edge Function deployment under the current managed backend. Generate database types only from the deployed schema; temporary typed RPC boundaries may be isolated in the new client until regeneration, not hand edits to generated types.\n+\n+Report four separate states: **code prepared**, **backend deployed**, **frontend switched**, **live acceptance verified**. Run ordinary authenticated two-device/outage/revocation acceptance for PT LR, PT RC, and break before expanding rollout. Later run standalone LR/RC separately. Keep #167 open while any supported active path lacks the contract or its acceptance evidence.\n*** End Patch"));

If the browser will not pause, she is told so\\r\\n   * and the section carries on running rather than half-pausing.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const pauseRun = useCallback(() => {\\r\\n    if (done || paused || countdown !== null || saving.current) return;\\r\\n    if (!pauseRecording()) {\\r\\n      setPauseError(\\r\\n        \\"This browser will not pause the recording, so the section cannot pause. Keep going, or leave and start again in Chrome.\\",\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    pausedSince.current = now();\\r\\n    pauseCount.current += 1;\\r\\n    setPauseError(null);\\r\\n    setPaused(true);\\r\\n  }, [countdown, done, paused, now, pauseRecording]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Back to it.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * The break is added to the total before the clock is let go, so the instant\\r\\n   * she resumes is the instant she left, and not one second of the break\\r\\n   * reaches her time or the file\'s index.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * A resume that fails leaves her paused on purpose. W2 stopped that being\\r\\n * silent. This is the way back.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Runs after the recordings, because the grade needs the audio to be in place\\r\\n * before it is asked for.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function recoverPendingRows(studentId: string): Promise<number> {\\r\\n  let recovered = 0;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    await purgeStaleRows();\\r\\n\\r\\n    const byRun = new Map<string, StashedRow[]>();\\r\\n    for (const row of await listStashedRows()) {\\r\\n      if (row.studentId !== studentId) continue;\\r\\n      // A run this tab is still recording is not a run to be repairing. Rows\\r\\n      // are only ever stashed at the end of one, so this is belt and braces.\\r\\n      if (isActive(row.runId)) continue;\\r\\n      const at = byRun.get(row.runId);\\r\\n      if (at) at.push(row);\\r\\n      else byRun.set(row.runId, [row]);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const [, rows] of byRun) {\\r\\n      let landed = 0;\\r\\n      for (const stashed of rows) {\\r\\n        /*\\r\\n         * One row per statement, and idempotent, exactly as the run\'s own\\r\\n         * backstop writes it. `ignoreDuplicates` means a row that did in fact\\r\\n         * arrive -- a response that succeeded after the tab gave up on it --\\r\\n         * is a success here rather than a conflict.\\r\\n         */\\r\\n        const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n          .from(\\"activity_item_responses\\")\\r\\n          .upsert([stashed.row as never], {\\r\\n            onConflict: \\"assignment_id,student_id,item_order,attempt_no\\",\\r\\n            ignoreDuplicates: true,\\r\\n          });\\r\\n        if (error) {\\r\\n          // Keep it. The next open tries again, and until then this copy is\\r\\n          // the only one that exists.\\r\\n          console.error(\\"[rrc] could not send a recovered answer\\", error);\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        await discardRow(stashed.key);\\r\\n        landed += 1;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      /*\\r\\n       * Ask for the grade only once the run is whole again.\\r\\n       *\\r\\n       * ADR 0027 refuses a run whose rows do not cover its section, so asking\\r\\n       * on a partial recovery buys a certain 409 -- which is the thing W2\\r\\n       * exists to stop happening in silence. `landed === rows.length` is the\\r\\n       * last row of that run landing, and nothing else.\\r\\n       */\\r\\n      if (landed > 0 && landed === rows.length) {\\r\\n        recovered += landed;\\r\\n        void requestSectionGrading(rows[0].assignmentId);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  } catch (e) {\\r\\n    console.error(\\"[rrc] answer recovery pass failed\\", e);\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ba03fc","wall_time_seconds":0.1816054,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":374,"output":"      ? {\\r\\n          numerator: assignment.score_numerator ?? 0,\\r\\n          judged: assignment.score_judged ?? items.length,\\r\\n          total: assignment.score_total ?? items.length,\\r\\n          percent: assignment.score,\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      : null;\\r\\n  const displayScore = storedScore ?? liveScore;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (progressLoadFailed) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"activity-centre min-h-screen p-8\\">\\r\\n      <p role=\\"alert\\">Could not load your saved progress. Neither is worth reporting.\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    backup.current = null;\\r\\n    stream.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());\\r\\n    recorder.current = null;\\r\\n    stream.current = null;\\r\\n    setRecording(false);\\r\\n    setPaused(false);\\r\\n    const blob = await finished;\\r\\n    return { blob, mimeType, durationMs, segments: segments.current.slice() };\\r\\n  }, [now]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * A recorder does not outlive the component that started it.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * `stop` is the run\'s own ending and the only place this used to close the\\r\\n   * microphone. A section she LEFT -- the exit button, a route change, any\\r\\n   * unmount before `done` -- left both recorders running with the microphone\\r\\n   * open, invisible, for as long as the tab lived. On 2026-09-07 one such\\r\\n   * orphan ran for two hours: through the student\'s real sitting of the same\\r\\n   * section and past its end, feeding its backup the whole time. When the tab\\r\\n   * finally reloaded, the recovery pass took that orphan\'s 116-minute file for\\r\\n   * a crashed run and uploaded it over the 66-minute recording the real run\\r\\n   * had stored, and the grader refused the section for a clock that sat 37\\r\\n   * minutes off.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * So the tracks close with the hook. Nothing is handed back: whatever this\\r\\n"}'}]

Hung off the run\\r\\n * record, the rows would be deleted by the success of the upload.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * So `discard` does not touch them and `purgeStaleRows` bounds them by their\\r\\n * own age instead, on the same seven days.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function stashRows(rows: Omit<StashedRow, \\"key\\" | \\"stashedAt\\">[], now = Date.now()): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!available() || !rows.length) return;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const instance = await db();\\r\\n    const tx = instance.transaction(ROWS, \\"readwrite\\");\\r\\n    await Promise.all([\\r\\n      ...rows.map((r) =>\\r\\n        tx.objectStore(ROWS).put({\\r\\n          ...r,\\r\\n          key: `${r.runId}:${String(r.itemOrder).padStart(4, \\"0\\")}`,\\r\\n          stashedAt: now,\\r\\n        } satisfies StashedRow),\\r\\n      ),\\r\\n      tx.done,\\r\\n    ]);\\r\\n  } catch (e) {\\r\\n    // She has finished twenty unbroken minutes. A stash that cannot be written\\r\\n    // leaves the run exactly as ungradable as it already was, and no worse.\\r\\n    console.error(\\"[rrc] could not keep a local copy of the unsaved answers\\", e);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Every row still waiting to be sent, across every run. */\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Found two material RRC issues:

1. `SectionRunner.tsx`: fresh closeout trusts duplicate-ignored upserts without exact-take readback. Failed attempt-number reads also default to 1, risking old-row/new-audio collisions. Require acknowledged attempt identity and verified rows/file before completion.
2. `activitiesClient.ts`: lost-response retries rewrite `completed_at`. Make completion conditional and acknowledge an already completed row without another update.

Root probe fixes now address KV identity, admin-role verification, and errors without codes. No edits.

Account evidence must still be verified before closeout. */\\r\\nexport function keepSectionCompletion(pending: PendingSectionCompletion): void {\\r\\n  localStorage.setItem(keyFor(pending.assignmentId, pending.studentId), JSON.stringify(pending));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function readSectionCompletion(assignmentId: string, studentId: string): PendingSectionCompletion | null {\\r\\n  const raw = localStorage.getItem(keyFor(assignmentId, studentId));\\r\\n  if (!raw) return null;\\r\\n  const pending = JSON.parse(raw) as PendingSectionCompletion;\\r\\n  if (pending.assignmentId !== assignmentId || pending.studentId !== studentId ||\\r\\n      !pending.audioPath || !Number.isInteger(pending.attemptNo) || pending.attemptNo < 1 ||\\r\\n      !Array.isArray(pending.itemOrders) || pending.itemOrders.length === 0 ||\\r\\n      !pending.itemOrders.every(Number.isInteger) || new Set(pending.itemOrders).size !== pending.itemOrders.length) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\\"The saved section closeout does not match this assignment.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return pending;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function requireSectionEvidence(pending: PendingSectionCompletion, rows: unknown[], itemOrders: number[]): void {\\r\\n  if (pending.itemOrders.length !== itemOrders.length || !itemOrders.every(order => pending.itemOrders.includes(order))) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\\"The saved section closeout does not match this section.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const saved = new Set(rows.flatMap(value => {\\r\\n    const row = value as { assignment_id?: string; student_id?: string; item_order?: number; attempt_no?: number; rrc?: { audio_path?: string } };\\r\\n    return row.assignment_id === pending.assignmentId && row.student_id === pending.studentId &&\\r\\n      row.attempt_no === pending.attemptNo && row.rrc?.audio_path === pending.audioPath ? Retry loading.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function requireSectionRecording(pending: PendingSectionCompletion): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const split = pending.audioPath.lastIndexOf(\\"/\\");\\r\\n  if (split < 1) throw new Error(\\"The saved recording path is unavailable.\\");\\r\\n  const filename = pending.audioPath.slice(split + 1);\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.storage.from(\\"drill-recordings\\")\\r\\n    .list(pending.audioPath.slice(0, split), { search: filename, limit: 100 });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  if (!data?.some(file => file.name === filename && file.id)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\\"Your section recording has not reached your account yet.\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Retry status alone; recordings and append-only responses are never replayed. */\\r\\nexport async function acknowledgeSectionCompletion(assignmentId: string, studentId: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  await completeSectionRun(assignmentId);\\r\\n  localStorage.removeItem(keyFor(assignmentId, studentId));\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"cb283e","wall_time_seconds":0.9252888,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":115,"output":"src/features/activity-centre/api/activitiesClient.ts:158:export async function markAssignmentStarted(assignmentId: string): Promise<void> {\\nsrc/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityRunner.tsx:10:  markAssignmentStarted,\\nsrc/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityRunner.tsx:594:          await markAssignmentStarted(assignment.id);\\nsrc/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityRunner.tsx:620:        await markAssignmentStarted(assignment.id);\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"87750e","wall_time_seconds":1.0571548,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":307,"output":"/**\\r\\n * PostgREST\'s two ways of saying \\"you named a column I do not have\\": the schema\\r\\n * cache miss on insert, and Postgres\'s own undefined_column. Either means the\\r\\n * telemetry migration has not been applied to this database yet.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction isUnknownColumn(error: { code?: string; message?: string } | null): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!error) return false;\\r\\n  return error.code === \\"PGRST204\\" || error.code === \\"42703\\";\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Student: write a verdict-bearing attempt to the append-only log.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The telemetry fields ride along on the same row: which judge decided the\\r\\n * verdict, what the world\'s structural classifier would have said, and how long\\r\\n * it took. They are measurement, never score, and they are strictly less\\r\\n * valuable than the attempt itself — so if the database has not been given\\r\\n * their columns yet, the attempt is written without them rather than lost.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function recordItemResponse(input: {\\r\\n  assignmentId: string;\\r\\n  studentId: string;\\r\\n  itemOrder: number;\\r\\n  responseText: string;\\r\\n  verdict: Verdict | null;\\r\\n  attemptNo: number;\\r\\n  judgeSource?: JudgeSource;\\r\\n  shadowFault?: string | null;\\r\\n  latencyMs?: number;\\r\\n  parserVersion?: string;\\r\\n}): Promise<void> {\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"831c26","wall_time_seconds":1.0214834,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1039,"output":"\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * The clock in the rail, ticked once a second.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * State rather than a ref, because it has to redraw. It is deliberately the\\r\\n   * only thing in this component that re-renders on a timer: nothing else on\\r\\n   * screen changes between her presses, and the recorder is untouched by it --\\r\\n   * `mark()` reads refs and the recorder is started once on mount.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * The ready beat, and the moment the section actually starts.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Every clock in the run is re-zeroed as it ends, so the three seconds are\\r\\n   * not charged to her goal. The RECORDER is deliberately not restarted: it\\r\\n   * has been running since mount, which is what lets her see the level move\\r\\n   * before the first stimulus, and restarting a recorder mid-section is the\\r\\n   * one thing the drill forbids outright. The beat simply sits at the head of\\r\\n   * the file, inside stimulus one\'s first window, as three seconds of nothing.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (countdown === null) return;\\r\\n    /*\\r\\n     * The beat is also the gate.\\r\\n     *\\r\\n     * `getUserMedia` is a prompt she has to answer, and answering it can take\\r\\n     * longer than three seconds, so a beat that ticks regardless would start\\r\\n     * the section before the microphone had said anything at all. It waits for\\r\\n     * an answer, and if the answer is no it holds here rather than spending\\r\\n     * twenty-five unrepeatable minutes on a run nothing can mark.\\r\\n     */\\r\\n    if (!recording && !micRefused) return;\\r\\n    if (micRefused) return;\\r\\n    if (countdown <= 0) {\\r\\n      startedAt.current = runNow();\\r\\n      phaseStartedAt.current = startedAt.current;\\r\\n      stimulusStartedAt.current = startedAt.current;\\r\\n      setCountdown(null);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const t = setTimeout(() => setCountdown((n) => (n === null ? null : n - 1)), 1000);\\r\\n    return () => clearTimeout(t);\\r\\n  }, [countdown, runNow, recording, micRefused]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * `countdown` is a dependency on purpose. This effect returns early during\\r\\n   * the beat, so the ticker can only start when the beat ends -- and the beat\\r\\n   * ending is a change to `countdown`. Without it the effect ran once, at\\r\\n   * mount, and the clock read 0:00 for the whole of every run.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (done || countdown !== null || paused) return;\\r\\n    const tick = setInterval(() => setElapsed(seconds(startedAt.current)), 1000);\\r\\n    return () => clearInterval(tick);\\r\\n  }, [countdown, done, paused, seconds]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * Hold the app still for the length of the run.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Two things in the browser will end a section without asking. The app\'s own\\r\\n   * freshness check reloads the tab when a newer bundle has been published, on\\r\\n   * every return to it -- so publishing mid-section and her glancing away was\\r\\n   * enough to lose twenty-five minutes. And a reflex Cmd-R or a closed tab does\\r\\n   * the same thing by hand.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * The first is suppressed outright: the reload is deferred until the run\\r\\n   * ends, which costs her one stale bundle for one section. The second cannot\\r\\n   * be suppressed, so it is questioned -- `beforeunload` is the browser\'s own\\r\\n   * prompt and the only guard available, and it is deliberately registered for\\r\\n   * the whole run rather than only once she has spoken, because the expensive\\r\\n   * thing to lose is the recording and that starts here.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const release = beginLiveRun();\\r\\n    const warn = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {\\r\\n      e.preventDefault();\\r\\n      // Set for the browsers that still require it; none of them show the\\r\\n      // string any more, and the prompt is generic by design.\\r\\n      e.returnValue = \\"\\";\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    window.addEventListener(\\"beforeunload\\", warn);\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      window.removeEventListener(\\"beforeunload\\", warn);\\r\\n      release();\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /*\\r\\n   * Press record at the very beginning. Before the first chunk, not on the\\r\\n   * first keypress: the drill\'s first act is reading, and a recording that\\r\\n   * starts on the first Space has already missed it.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!attemptReady) return;\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"e14068","wall_time_seconds":0.8587535,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":36,"output":"docs/implementation-plans\\\\2026-09-30-activity-revocation-readiness.md\\ndocs/implementation-plans\\\\artifacts\\\\2026-09-30-activity-revocation.sql\\n"}'}]

public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') then\\r\\n      raise exception \'Admin access is required to revoke an Activity\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n    end if;\\r\\n    -- Never accept client-supplied actor attribution or timestamps.\\r\\n    if NEW.revoked_by is distinct from auth.uid() then\\r\\n      raise exception \'Revocation actor must be the authenticated admin\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n    end if;\\r\\n    NEW.revoked_at := statement_timestamp();\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  return NEW;\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\ncreate trigger activity_revocation_guard before insert or update\\r\\n  on public.activity_assignments for each row execute function public.guard_activity_revocation();\\r\\n\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"Students read own activity assignments\\" on
