revoked_at is null);\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"Students update own activity assignments\\" on public.activity_assignments;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"Students update own activity assignments\\" on public.activity_assignments\\r\\n  for update to authenticated using (student_id = auth.uid() and revoked_at is null)\\r\\n  with check (student_id = auth.uid() and revoked_at is null and revoked_by is null);\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Status remains unchanged, so exclude revoked sources from the live-run index.\\r\\ndrop index if exists public.activity_assignments_active_unique;\\r\\ncreate unique index activity_assignments_active_unique\\r\\n  on public.activity_assignments(student_id, activity_id)\\r\\n  where status in (\'assigned\', \'in_progress\') and activity_id is not null and revoked_at is null;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.guard_activity_response_access()\\r\\nreturns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$\\r\\ndeclare v_student uuid; v_revoked timestamptz;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  -- Serialize against the revocation RPC\'s FOR UPDATE lock. A response either\\r\\n  -- lands before revocation or is rejected after it, including recovery writes.\\r\\n  select student_id, revoked_at into v_student, v_revoked\\r\\n    from public.activity_assignments where id = NEW.assignment_id for share;\\r\\n  if not found or v_student is distinct from NEW.student_id or v_revoked is not null then\\r\\n    raise exception \'Activity assignment is unavailable for new responses\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  return NEW;\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\ncreate trigger activity_response_access_guard before insert\\r\\n  on public.activity_item_responses for each row execute function public.guard_activity_response_access();\\r\\ndrop policy if exists \\"activity_item_responses: owner can insert\\" on public.activity_item_responses;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"activity_item_responses: owner can insert\\" on public.activity_item_responses\\r\\n  for insert to authenticated with check (\\r\\n    auth.uid() = student_id and exists (\\r\\n      select 1 from public.activity_assignments a where a.id = assignment_id\\r\\n        and a.student_id = auth.uid() and a.revoked_at is null\\r\\n    )\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Prevent a later mirror insert/unhide from resurrecting a revoked source.\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.guard_activity_mirror_access()\\r\\nreturns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$\\r\\ndeclare v_revoked timestamptz; v_student uuid;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  if NEW.visible_to_student and NEW.source_id is not null and\\r\\n     (NEW.source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or NEW.type = \'activity\') then\\r\\n    select revoked_at, student_id into v_revoked, v_student\\r\\n      from public.activity_assignments where id::text = NEW.source_id for share;\\r\\n    if not found or v_revoked is not null or v_student is distinct from NEW.student_id then\\r\\n      raise exception \'Activity mirror must name an available source for this student\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n    end if;\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  return NEW;\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\ncreate trigger activity_mirror_access_guard before insert or update\\r\\n  on public.classroom_items for each row execute function public.guard_activity_mirror_access();\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.activity_revocation_capability()\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  if auth.uid() is null or not public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') then\\r\\n    raise exception \'Admin access is required\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  return jsonb_build_object(\'version\', 1, \'source_preserving\', true);\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.revoke_activity_assignment(p_assignment_id uuid, p_student_id uuid)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public as $$\\r\\ndeclare v_actor uuid := auth.uid(); v_row public.activity_assignments%rowtype;\\r\\n  v_already boolean; v_mirrors integer;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  if v_actor is null or not public.has_role(v_actor, \'admin\') then\\r\\n    raise exception \'Admin access is required\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  select * into v_row from public.activity_assignments\\r\\n    where id = p_assignment_id and student_id = p_student_id for update;\\r\\n  if not found then\\r\\n    raise exception \'Activity assignment does not belong to this student\' using errcode = \'P0002\';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  v_already := v_row.revoked_at is not null;\\r\\n  if not v_already then\\r\\n    update public.activity_assignments set revoked_at = statement_timestamp(), revoked_by = v_actor\\r\\n      where id = v_row.id returning * into v_row;\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  -- Hide every mirror, without deleting its content, metadata or progress.\\r\\n  update public.classroom_items set visible_to_student = false\\r\\n    where source_id = v_row.id::text\\r\\n      and (source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or type = \'activity\')\\r\\n      and visible_to_student;\\r\\n  get diagnostics v_mirrors = row_count;\\r\\n  return jsonb_build_object(\'assignment_id\', v_row.id, \'student_id\', v_row.student_id,\\r\\n    \'revoked_at\', v_row.revoked_at, \'revoked_by\', v_row.revoked_by,\\r\\n    \'performed_by\', v_actor, \'already_revoked\', v_already, \'mirrors_hidden\', v_mirrors);\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\nrevoke all on function public.activity_revocation_capability() from public, anon;\\r\\nrevoke all on function public.revoke_activity_assignment(uuid, uuid) from public, anon;\\r\\ngrant execute on function public.activity_revocation_capability() to authenticated;\\r\\ngrant execute on function public.revoke_activity_assignment(uuid, uuid) to authenticated;\\r\\ncommit;\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"bf8db6","wall_time_seconds":1.0271159,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1607,"output":"# Activity Unassign needs a source revocation transaction\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is a deployment draft. The live backend has no Activity revocation columns or RPC, and current teacher controls correctly keep Activity Unassign unavailable. Nothing in this slice changes live records or enables those controls.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe artifact at `artifacts/2026-09-30-activity-revocation.sql` adds independent `revoked_at` and `revoked_by`. Progress status, score, timestamps, snapshots, original `assigned_by`, append-only responses, grades, and mirror metadata remain intact. Revocation is permanent in this version. Reassignment creates a new issued instance; it does not restore the revoked source.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The server owns the actor and the transaction\\r\\n\\r\\n`revoke_activity_assignment(p_assignment_id, p_student_id)` requires an authenticated admin, locks the expected student\'s source row, stamps the actor from `auth.uid()`, and hides every linked Activity mirror in the same transaction. It returns the source/student IDs, original revocation actor/timestamp, this request\'s actor, whether the source was already revoked, and the count of hidden mirrors. A second admin\'s retry keeps the first admin\'s attribution. There is no caller-provided actor parameter. The original teacher attribution is not rewritten.\\r\\n\\r\\nExisting issuance in `src/lib/assignWork.ts` writes `assigned_by: teacherId` and mirror `created_by: teacherId`. The assignment INSERT policy requires `assigned_by = auth.uid()`. Existing `start_section_attempt` uses the Activity definition\'s `created_by` because the student is choosing a library attempt, not receiving a new teacher assignment. Do not conflate that author with the actor who later revokes the issued run. Broad existing admin UPDATE permits unrelated source edits; this draft only adds authenticated revocation attribution and prevents all changes to already-revoked assignments.\\r\\n\\r\\nAtomic source-and-mirror revocation is guaranteed through this RPC. Existing broad admin UPDATE still allows a raw admin edit to set correctly attributed revocation fields without hiding mirrors; that edit is outside the supported operation and must not be used by the client. Existing admin DELETE privileges also remain unchanged. History preservation describes this RPC\'s behavior, not a guarantee that a privileged administrator can never delete records. Restricting those broader privileges would be a separate migration with a wider compatibility review.\\r\\n\\r\\nStudent source SELECT and UPDATE exclude revoked assignments, including direct API requests. Student response INSERT requires an available source owned by the same account. A response trigger locks the source with `FOR SHARE`, serializing recovery/insert against revocation\'s `FOR UPDATE`; even a service-role recovery insert cannot append evidence to a revoked source. Visible mirror inserts or unhides require an available source belonging to that mirror\'s student. Existing history remains stored for admin review. Hiding a mirror alone is still insufficient.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Wire the UI only after deployment verification\\r\\n\\r\\n`src/features/activity-centre/api/activityRevocation.ts` probes `activity_revocation_capability()`. Missing RPC, denied access, bad version, or network failure all return unavailable. The helper checks the current authenticated actor, passes the exact source/student IDs, and requires a matching receipt. It is deliberately not wired to a UI control in this slice.\\r\\n\\r\\nAfter deployment, add `revoked_at`/`revoked_by` to the assignment domain types and summary reader in `activitiesClient.ts`; generated Supabase types must be regenerated rather than hand-edited. Admin My Work/Dossier readers still see revoked source rows, so filter them from actionable projections or show a separate read-only historical row. `ActivityRunner.tsx` needs an early revocation gate for an admin viewing their own revoked assignment, because admin SELECT intentionally still sees the source; students already get no source row through RLS. Keep teacher historical inspection read-only. A mounted student\'s stale runner will fail source completion and response writes server-side after revocation.\\r\\n\\r\\nEnable controls in `HubAssignments.tsx` and `pages/teacher/students/DossierWork.tsx` only when the capability probe confirms version 1. Require the existing confirmation dialog, call the helper with the issued source ID and selected student ID, and refresh both the mirror query and scoped source feed after a confirmed receipt. Do not delete the Classroom mirror or the source row. An error must keep the record visible and must not say Unassigned. Ensure absent-mirror Activity instances also have the source control.\\r\\n\\r\\nBefore deployment, apply the SQL through the approved Lovable Cloud backend route and verify the deployed catalog/policies. The local harness below uses only synthetic fixtures and is not proof that the live backend has been upgraded. Check existing policy names against the live catalog before installation: PostgreSQL permissive policies combine with OR, so an additional broad student policy would bypass the replacements. Test actual student and admin JWTs on the deployed backend without modifying real student assignments.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Run the SQL in an isolated local database\\r\\n\\r\\n`scripts/verification/activity-revocation-sql.mjs` accepts `--pglite-module` or `PGLITE_MODULE`, points at an externally installed PGlite runtime, creates synthetic auth/admin/student fixtures in memory, and applies the draft SQL. It exercises denied student calls and forged attribution, owner mutation denial, atomic rollback when mirror hiding fails, retained source/response/grade history, idempotence across two admins, blocked mirror resurrection, direct student reads/writes, service-level recovery denial, and new issuance after revocation. No runtime dependency was added to the app.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe client Vitest suite checks capability failure, no mutation against an unsupported backend, expected subject/actor receipt validation, and idempotent attribution. These are contract checks; the executable SQL harness is the database behavior check.\\r\\n\\r\\nKnown boundary: a student with broad Activity library access can choose a new library run. Revoking one issued source is not withdrawal of their entire Activity library entitlement. Audio storage and historical grade reads are not deleted or revoked by this source access operation. Those are separate product decisions.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"0676e2","wall_time_seconds":1.5701161,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":127764,"output":"Warning:
